# Regional keys

Generated by `tools/regional_keys.py` on 2026-11-17. Each key is a share of a national quantity by NUTS-3 region; the 19 shares of a key sum to exactly 1 (checked with exact rational arithmetic) and the regional total is checked against the national figure of the same source.

Åland (FI200) is a normal region of the model, connected to the rest of Finland by links.
